"use client";
import React from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import Link from "next/link";
import Image from "next/image";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const transition = {
  type: "spring",
  mass: 0.5,
  damping: 11.5,
  stiffness: 100,
  restDelta: 0.001,
  restSpeed: 0.001,
};

export const MenuItem = ({
  setActive,
  active,
  item,
  children,
}: {
  setActive: (item: string) => void;
  active: string | null;
  item: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}) => {
  return (
    <div onMouseEnter={() => setActive(item)} className="relative">
      <motion.p
        transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
        className="cursor-pointer text-[14px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:text-ink"
      >
        {item}
      </motion.p>
      {active !== null && (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.85, y: 10 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={transition}
        >
          {active === item && (
            <div className="absolute top-[calc(100%_+_1.2rem)] left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 pt-4">
              <motion.div
                transition={transition}
                layoutId="active"
                className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-card shadow-xl backdrop-blur-sm"
              >
                <motion.div layout className="h-full w-max p-4">
                  {children}
                </motion.div>
              </motion.div>
            </div>
          )}
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export const Menu = ({
  setActive,
  children,
}: {
  setActive: (item: string | null) => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) => {
  return (
    <nav
      onMouseLeave={() => setActive(null)}
      className="relative flex items-center justify-center gap-6"
    >
      {children}
    </nav>
  );
};

export const ProductItem = ({
  title,
  description,
  href,
  src,
}: {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  href: string;
  src: string;
}) => {
  return (
    <Link href={href} className="flex gap-3">
      <Image
        src={src}
        width={128}
        height={64}
        alt={title}
        className="h-16 w-32 shrink-0 rounded-md object-cover shadow-lg"
      />
      <div>
        <h4 className="mb-1 text-[14.5px] font-semibold text-ink">{title}</h4>
        <p className="max-w-[10rem] text-[12.5px] leading-snug text-ink-soft">{description}</p>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
};

type HoveredLinkProps = React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof Link> & {
  className?: string;
};

export const HoveredLink = ({ children, className, ...rest }: HoveredLinkProps) => {
  return (
    <Link
      {...rest}
      className={cn("text-[13.5px] text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:text-ink", className)}
    >
      {children}
    </Link>
  );
};

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\pricing-tiers.tsx

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Check } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export type PricingPlan = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  price: string;
  frequency?: string;
  description: string;
  features: string[];
  buttonText: string;
  buttonLink: string;
  isFeatured?: boolean;
  badge?: string;
};

export type PricingSectionData = {
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  plans: PricingPlan[];
};

function PricingTier({
  name,
  price,
  frequency,
  description,
  features,
  buttonText,
  buttonLink,
  isFeatured = false,
  badge
}: PricingPlan) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col rounded-card border p-8 transition-transform duration-200",
        isFeatured
          ? "border-accent-violet/50 bg-gold-tint shadow-xl ring-1 ring-accent-violet/30 md:-translate-y-2"
          : "border-line bg-card shadow-sm hover:-translate-y-1"
      )}
    >
      {isFeatured && badge && (
        <span className="mb-3 w-fit self-center rounded-full bg-gold-dark px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-white">
          {badge}
        </span>
      )}

      <h3 className="text-center font-display text-[22px] font-bold text-ink">{name}</h3>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-baseline justify-center gap-1">
        <span className="font-mono text-[42px] font-extrabold leading-none text-ink">{price}</span>
        {frequency && <span className="text-[14px] font-medium text-ink-soft">{frequency}</span>}
      </div>

      <p className="mt-4 text-center text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">{description}</p>

      <ul className="mt-8 flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3.5">
        {features.map((feature) => (
          <li key={feature} className="flex items-start gap-3 text-[13.5px] text-ink">
            <span
              className={cn(
                "mt-0.5 flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full",
                isFeatured ? "bg-accent-violet text-white" : "bg-teal-light text-teal"
              )}
            >
              <Check size={12} strokeWidth={3} />
            </span>
            {feature}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>

      <Link
        href={buttonLink}
        className={cn(
          "mt-8 w-full rounded-full py-3 text-center text-[13.5px] font-semibold transition-transform hover:scale-[1.02]",
          isFeatured ? "bg-ink text-paper" : "border border-line text-ink hover:bg-paper-dim"
        )}
      >
        {buttonText}
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

export function PricingSection({
  data,
  className,
  header
}: {
  data: PricingSectionData;
  className?: string;
  header?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className={className}>
      {header ?? (
        <div className="mb-10 text-center">
          <h2 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink md:text-[32px]">{data.title}</h2>
          {data.subtitle && (
            <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-xl text-[14.5px] text-ink-soft">{data.subtitle}</p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="flex flex-col items-stretch justify-center gap-6 md:flex-row md:items-center">
        {data.plans.map((plan) => (
          <PricingTier key={plan.id} {...plan} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default PricingSection;

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\search-command.tsx

"use client";

/**
 * SearchCommand
 * cmdk-based command palette that powers the TopSearchBar dropdown.
 * Keyboard-navigable, grouped by content type, zero custom CSS hacks.
 */
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Command } from "cmdk";
import Link from "next/link";
import {
  GraduationCap,
  Newspaper,
  BookOpen,
  Users,
  Award,
  ArrowUpRight,
  Search
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export type SearchGroup = {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  icon: ReactNode;
  items: { id: string; title: string; href: string; sub?: string }[];
};

type SearchCommandProps = {
  query: string;
  groups: SearchGroup[];
  onClose: () => void;
  className?: string;
};

export function SearchCommand({ query, groups, onClose, className }: SearchCommandProps) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // close on outside click
  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClick(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClick);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClick);
  }, [onClose]);

  const totalItems = groups.reduce((s, g) => s + g.items.length, 0);
  const isEmpty = totalItems === 0;

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "absolute left-0 right-0 top-[calc(100%+8px)] z-50 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line bg-card shadow-2xl",
        className
      )}
    >
      <Command className="w-full" shouldFilter={false}>
        <Command.List className="max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto p-1.5">
          {isEmpty ? (
            <Command.Empty className="py-10 text-center text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
              {query.trim()
                ? `No results for "${query}"`
                : "Start typing to search across courses, news, blog, and more."}
            </Command.Empty>
          ) : (
            groups.map((group) => {
              if (group.items.length === 0) return null;
              return (
                <Command.Group
                  key={group.key}
                  heading={
                    <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 pt-2 pb-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-ink-soft/60">
                      {group.icon}
                      {group.label}
                    </div>
                  }
                >
                  {group.items.map((item) => (
                    <Command.Item
                      key={item.id}
                      value={item.id}
                      asChild
                      className="cursor-pointer rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] transition-colors aria-selected:bg-gold-tint aria-selected:text-ink"
                      onSelect={() => onClose()}
                    >
                      <Link
                        href={item.href}
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 outline-none"
                      >
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <p className="truncate font-medium text-ink">{item.title}</p>
                          {item.sub && (
                            <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-[11.5px] text-ink-soft">{item.sub}</p>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <ArrowUpRight size={13} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft/60" />
                      </Link>
                    </Command.Item>
                  ))}
                </Command.Group>
              );
            })
          )}

          {/* footer: see all */}
          {query.trim() && (
            <div className="border-t border-line mt-1 pt-1">
              <Command.Item
                value="__see_all__"
                asChild
                className="cursor-pointer rounded-lg aria-selected:bg-gold-tint"
                onSelect={onClose}
              >
                <Link
                  href={`/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`}
                  onClick={onClose}
                  className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-accent-violet outline-none hover:underline"
                >
                  <Search size={13} />
                  See all results for &quot;{query}&quot;
                </Link>
              </Command.Item>
            </div>
          )}
        </Command.List>
      </Command>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import * as SeparatorPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-separator"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

type SeparatorProps = React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof SeparatorPrimitive.Root>

const Separator = React.forwardRef<React.ElementRef<typeof SeparatorPrimitive.Root>, SeparatorProps>(
  function Separator({ className, orientation = "horizontal", decorative = true, ...props }, ref) {
    return (
      <SeparatorPrimitive.Root
        ref={ref}
        decorative={decorative}
        orientation={orientation}
        className={cn(
          "shrink-0 bg-line",
          orientation === "horizontal" ? "h-[1px] w-full" : "h-full w-[1px]",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      />
    )
  }
)

export { Separator }

export type CategoryDef = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
};

export const CATEGORIES: CategoryDef[] = [
  { id: "ai-fundamentals", name: "AI Fundamentals & Prompt Engineering", slug: "ai-fundamentals" },
  { id: "ai-marketing", name: "AI for Marketing & Sales", slug: "ai-marketing" },
  { id: "ai-automation", name: "AI Automation & Workflow Tools", slug: "ai-automation" },
  { id: "ai-support", name: "AI Customer Support & Communication", slug: "ai-support" },
  { id: "ai-dev", name: "AI Website & App Development", slug: "ai-dev" },
  { id: "ai-integration", name: "AI Integration for Developers", slug: "ai-integration" },
  { id: "ai-design", name: "AI Design & Creative Tools", slug: "ai-design" },
  { id: "ai-career", name: "AI for Careers & Job Search", slug: "ai-career" }
];

export function getCategoryBySlug(slug: string): CategoryDef | undefined {
  return CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.slug === slug);
}

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

const LEARNER_PREFIX = "/dashboard";
const INSTRUCTOR_PREFIX = "/instructor";

export function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  const sessionCookie = request.cookies.get("saachi_session");

  const isProtected =
    pathname.startsWith(LEARNER_PREFIX) || pathname.startsWith(INSTRUCTOR_PREFIX);

  if (isProtected && !sessionCookie) {
    const loginUrl = new URL("/login", request.url);
    loginUrl.searchParams.set("redirect", pathname);
    return NextResponse.redirect(loginUrl);
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/dashboard/:path*", "/instructor/:path*"]
};

    ignoreBuildErrors: false // re-enabled by Fix-SAI2-Issues.ps1

    ignoreDuringBuilds: false // re-enabled by Fix-SAI2-Issues.ps1
  },

    remotePatterns: [
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "**.cloudfront.net" },
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "res.cloudinary.com" },
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "picsum.photos" },
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "images.unsplash.com" },

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Accordion.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type AccordionItemData = {
  id: string;
  title: ReactNode;
  content: ReactNode;
  meta?: ReactNode;
};

export type AccordionProps = {
  items: AccordionItemData[];
  defaultOpenId?: string;
  className?: string;
};

export function Accordion({ items, defaultOpenId, className }: AccordionProps) {
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(defaultOpenId ?? null);

  return (
    <div className={cn("flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line divide-y divide-line", className)}>
      {items.map((item) => {
        const isOpen = openId === item.id;
        return (
          <div key={item.id} className="bg-card">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOpenId(isOpen ? null : item.id)}
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-4 text-left"
              aria-expanded={isOpen}
            >
              <div className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-3">
                <span className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{item.title}</span>
                {item.meta}
              </div>
              <ChevronDown
                size={18}
                className={cn(
                  "shrink-0 text-ink-soft transition-transform duration-200 ease-out",
                  isOpen && "rotate-180 text-gold-dark"
                )}
              />
            </button>
            <div
              className={cn(
                "grid transition-all duration-200 ease-out",
                isOpen ? "grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100" : "grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0"
              )}
            >
              <div className="overflow-hidden">
                <div className="px-5 pb-4 text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">{item.content}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

type BadgeVariant = "default" | "success" | "warning" | "error" | "gold";

export type BadgeProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement> & {
  variant?: BadgeVariant;
};

const variantStyles: Record<BadgeVariant, string> = {
  default: "bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft",
  success: "bg-teal-light text-teal",
  warning: "bg-gold-tint text-gold-dark",
  error: "bg-error-tint text-error",
  gold: "bg-gold text-ink"
};

export function Badge({ className, variant = "default", children, ...props }: BadgeProps) {
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold",
        variantStyles[variant],
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

import { forwardRef, type ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger" | "brand";
type ButtonSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";

export type ButtonProps = ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  loading?: boolean;
};

const variantStyles: Record<ButtonVariant, string> = {
  primary: "bg-gold text-ink hover:bg-gold-dark",
  secondary: "border border-line-strong bg-card text-ink hover:border-ink",
  ghost: "bg-transparent text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim",
  danger: "bg-error text-white hover:brightness-90",
    brand: "bg-gradient-brand text-white hover:brightness-110 shadow-[0_0_40px_-12px_rgba(124,58,237,0.5)]"
};

const sizeStyles: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
  sm: "h-9 px-4 text-sm",
  md: "h-11 px-5 text-[15px]",
  lg: "h-[52px] px-7 text-[16px]"
};

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant = "primary", size = "md", loading, disabled, children, ...props }, ref) => {
    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        disabled={disabled || loading}
        className={cn(
          "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em]",
          "transition-colors duration-150 ease-out disabled:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none",
          variantStyles[variant],
          sizeStyles[size],
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {loading ? "Workingâ€¦" : children}
      </button>
    );
  }
);
Button.displayName = "Button";

import { forwardRef, type InputHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { Check } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type CheckboxProps = Omit<InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, "type"> & {
  label?: string;
};

export const Checkbox = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, CheckboxProps>(
  ({ className, label, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const checkboxId = id ?? props.name;

    return (
      <label htmlFor={checkboxId} className="inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2.5 select-none">
        <span className="relative inline-flex h-[18px] w-[18px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center">
          <input
            ref={ref}
            type="checkbox"
            id={checkboxId}
            className={cn("peer absolute inset-0 h-full w-full cursor-pointer opacity-0", className)}
            {...props}
          />
          <span className="flex h-[18px] w-[18px] items-center justify-center rounded-[5px] border border-line-strong bg-card transition-colors duration-150 ease-out peer-checked:border-gold peer-checked:bg-gold peer-focus-visible:ring-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-gold/40">
            <Check size={13} strokeWidth={3} className="text-ink opacity-0 peer-checked:opacity-100" />
          </span>
        </span>
        {label && <span className="text-[14px] text-ink">{label}</span>}
      </label>
    );
  }
);
Checkbox.displayName = "Checkbox";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Star } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type CourseCardProps = {
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  price: number;
  currency?: string;
  rating: number;
  ratingCount: number;
  className?: string;
};

export function CourseCard({
  slug,
  title,
  thumbnailUrl,
  instructorName,
  price,
  currency = "USD",
  rating,
  ratingCount,
  className
}: CourseCardProps) {
  const formattedPrice = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    minimumFractionDigits: price % 1 === 0 ? 0 : 2
  }).format(price);

  return (
    <Link
      href={`/courses/${slug}`}
      className={cn(
        "group flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card",
        "transition-all duration-200 ease-out hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-gold-dark/40",
        className
      )}
    >
      <div className="relative aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden bg-paper-dim">
        {thumbnailUrl ? (
          // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
          <img
            src={thumbnailUrl}
            alt={title}
            className="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 ease-out group-hover:scale-[1.02]"
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center text-[13px] text-ink-soft/60">
            No preview
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2 p-4">
        <h3 className="line-clamp-2 text-[15px] font-semibold leading-snug text-ink">{title}</h3>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{instructorName}</p>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px]">
          <Star size={14} className="fill-gold text-gold" />
          <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{rating.toFixed(1)}</span>
          <span className="text-ink-soft/60">({ratingCount.toLocaleString()})</span>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-auto pt-2 font-mono text-[17px] font-semibold text-ink">{formattedPrice}</div>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\DataTable.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { ChevronUp, ChevronDown, ChevronsUpDown } from "lucide-react";
import {
  flexRender,
  getCoreRowModel,
  getPaginationRowModel,
  getSortedRowModel,
  useReactTable,
  type ColumnDef,
  type SortingState
} from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useState } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";
import { ShimmerTable } from "./Shimmer";
import { EmptyState } from "./EmptyState";
import { Button } from "./Button";

export type DataTableProps<T> = {
  columns: ColumnDef<T, any>[];
  data: T[];
  isLoading?: boolean;
  emptyTitle?: string;
  emptyDescription?: string;
  pageSize?: number;
  toolbar?: ReactNode;
  onRowClick?: (row: T) => void;
};

export function DataTable<T>({
  columns,
  data,
  isLoading,
  emptyTitle = "Nothing here yet",
  emptyDescription,
  pageSize = 10,
  toolbar,
  onRowClick
}: DataTableProps<T>) {
  const [sorting, setSorting] = useState<SortingState>([]);

  const table = useReactTable({
    data,
    columns,
    state: { sorting },
    onSortingChange: setSorting,
    getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),
    getSortedRowModel: getSortedRowModel(),
    getPaginationRowModel: getPaginationRowModel(),
    initialState: { pagination: { pageSize } }
  });

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {toolbar}
        <ShimmerTable rows={6} columns={columns.length} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {toolbar}
        <EmptyState title={emptyTitle} description={emptyDescription} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      {toolbar}
      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
            <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim">
              {table.getHeaderGroups().map((headerGroup) => (
                <tr key={headerGroup.id}>
                  {headerGroup.headers.map((header) => {
                    const sortState = header.column.getIsSorted();
                    return (
                      <th key={header.id} className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">
                        {header.isPlaceholder ? null : (
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={header.column.getToggleSortingHandler()}
                            className={cn(
                              "flex items-center gap-1.5",
                              header.column.getCanSort() ? "cursor-pointer select-none hover:text-ink" : "cursor-default"
                            )}
                          >
                            {flexRender(header.column.columnDef.header, header.getContext())}
                            {header.column.getCanSort() &&
                              (sortState === "asc" ? (
                                <ChevronUp size={13} />
                              ) : sortState === "desc" ? (
                                <ChevronDown size={13} />
                              ) : (
                                <ChevronsUpDown size={13} className="opacity-40" />
                              ))}
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </th>
                    );
                  })}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {table.getRowModel().rows.map((row) => (
                <tr
                  key={row.id}
                  onClick={() => onRowClick?.(row.original)}
                  className={cn(
                    "border-b border-line last:border-0",
                    onRowClick && "cursor-pointer transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
                  )}
                >
                  {row.getVisibleCells().map((cell) => (
                    <td key={cell.id} className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3.5 text-ink">
                      {flexRender(cell.column.columnDef.cell, cell.getContext())}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {table.getPageCount() > 1 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-1">
          <span className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            Page {table.getState().pagination.pageIndex + 1} of {table.getPageCount()}
          </span>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => table.previousPage()} disabled={!table.getCanPreviousPage()}>
              Previous
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => table.nextPage()} disabled={!table.getCanNextPage()}>
              Next
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Inbox } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type EmptyStateProps = {
  icon?: ReactNode;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
};

export function EmptyState({ icon, title, description, action, className }: EmptyStateProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-card border border-dashed border-line-strong px-8 py-16 text-center",
        className
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold-tint text-gold-dark">
        {icon ?? <Inbox size={22} />}
      </div>
      <h3 className="font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">{title}</h3>
      {description && <p className="max-w-[420px] text-[14px] text-ink-soft">{description}</p>}
      {action && <div className="mt-2">{action}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Checkbox } from "./Checkbox";
import { Select } from "./Select";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type FilterSidebarValue = {
  categories: string[];
  level?: string;
  language?: string;
};

export type FilterSidebarProps = {
  categoryOptions: { label: string; value: string }[];
  levelOptions: { label: string; value: string }[];
  languageOptions: { label: string; value: string }[];
  value: FilterSidebarValue;
  onChange: (next: FilterSidebarValue) => void;
  className?: string;
};

export function FilterSidebar({
  categoryOptions,
  levelOptions,
  languageOptions,
  value,
  onChange,
  className
}: FilterSidebarProps) {
  const toggleCategory = (cat: string) => {
    const isActive = value.categories.includes(cat);
    const nextCategories = isActive
      ? value.categories.filter((c) => c !== cat)
      : [...value.categories, cat];
    onChange({ ...value, categories: nextCategories });
  };

  return (
    <aside className={cn("flex w-full flex-col gap-8 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6", className)}>
      <div>
        <h4 className="mb-3 text-[13px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ink-soft/70">
          Category
        </h4>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
          {categoryOptions.map((opt) => (
            <Checkbox
              key={opt.value}
              label={opt.label}
              checked={value.categories.includes(opt.value)}
              onChange={() => toggleCategory(opt.value)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <Select
        label="Skill level"
        placeholder="All levels"
        options={levelOptions}
        value={value.level ?? ""}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, level: e.target.value || undefined })}
      />

      <Select
        label="Language"
        placeholder="All languages"
        options={languageOptions}
        value={value.language ?? ""}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, language: e.target.value || undefined })}
      />

      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => onChange({ categories: [], level: undefined, language: undefined })}
        className="text-left text-[13px] font-medium text-gold-dark hover:underline"
      >
        Clear all filters
      </button>
    </aside>
  );
}

import { forwardRef, type InputHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type InputProps = InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> & {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
};

export const Input = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(
  ({ className, label, error, helperText, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const inputId = id ?? props.name;

    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={inputId} className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <input
          ref={ref}
          id={inputId}
          className={cn(
            "h-11 w-full rounded-control border border-line-strong bg-card px-3.5 text-[15px] text-ink",
            "placeholder:text-ink-soft/50 outline-none transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
            "focus:border-gold",
            error && "border-error focus:border-error",
            className
          )}
          aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
          {...props}
        />
        {error ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{error}</p>
        ) : helperText ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{helperText}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }
);
Input.displayName = "Input";

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Loading.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Lottie from "lottie-react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type LoadingProps = {
  size?: number;
  label?: string;
  fullScreen?: boolean;
  className?: string;
};

export function Loading({ size = 120, label, fullScreen = false, className }: LoadingProps) {
  const [animationData, setAnimationData] = useState<object | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch("/animations/loading.json")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (!cancelled) setAnimationData(data);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        // Silently ignore Ã¢â‚¬â€ falls back to just showing the label below.
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3",
        fullScreen ? "min-h-[60vh] w-full" : "py-10",
        className
      )}
      role="status"
      aria-live="polite"
    >
      {animationData ? (
        <Lottie animationData={animationData} loop autoplay style={{ width: size, height: size }} />
      ) : (
        <div
          className="animate-pulse rounded-full border-2 border-line-strong border-t-gold"
          style={{ width: size / 2, height: size / 2 }}
        />
      )}
      {label && <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{label}</p>}
      <span className="sr-only">Loading</span>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Modal.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { type ReactNode, useEffect } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type ModalProps = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  footer?: ReactNode;
  size?: "md" | "lg";
};

export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, children, footer, size = "md" }: ModalProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div
        className={cn(
          "relative w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6",
          size === "lg" ? "max-w-[720px]" : "max-w-[520px]"
        )}
      >
        {title && (
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="font-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">{title}</h2>
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              aria-label="Close"
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:bg-paper-dim"
            >
              <X size={18} />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
        <div>{children}</div>
        {footer && <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-3">{footer}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Star, BadgeCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type ReviewCardProps = {
  authorName: string;
  authorAvatarUrl?: string;
  rating: number;
  comment: string;
  createdAt: string;
  verifiedPurchase?: boolean;
  className?: string;
};

export function ReviewCard({
  authorName,
  authorAvatarUrl,
  rating,
  comment,
  createdAt,
  verifiedPurchase,
  className
}: ReviewCardProps) {
  const formattedDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    year: "numeric",
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric"
  }).format(new Date(createdAt));

  return (
    <div className={cn("rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6", className)}>
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-1">
        {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
          <Star key={i} size={15} className={i < Math.round(rating) ? "fill-gold text-gold" : "text-line-strong"} />
        ))}
      </div>

      <p className="mb-4 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-ink">{comment}</p>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-teal-light text-[13px] font-semibold text-teal">
          {authorAvatarUrl ? (
            // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
            <img src={authorAvatarUrl} alt={authorName} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
          ) : (
            authorName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()
          )}
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{authorName}</span>
            {verifiedPurchase && <BadgeCheck size={14} className="text-teal" />}
          </div>
          <span className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft/60">{formattedDate}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { forwardRef, type SelectHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";

export type SelectOption = {
  label: string;
  value: string;
};

export type SelectProps = SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> & {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
  options: SelectOption[];
  placeholder?: string;
};

export const Select = forwardRef<HTMLSelectElement, SelectProps>(
  ({ className, label, error, helperText, options, placeholder, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const selectId = id ?? props.name;

    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={selectId} className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <div className="relative">
          <select
            ref={ref}
            id={selectId}
            className={cn(
              "h-11 w-full appearance-none rounded-control border border-line-strong bg-card px-3.5 pr-10 text-[15px] text-ink",
              "outline-none transition-colors duration-150 ease-out focus:border-gold",
              error && "border-error focus:border-error",
              className
            )}
            aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
            {...props}
          >
            {placeholder && (
              <option value="" disabled>
                {placeholder}
              </option>
            )}
            {options.map((opt) => (
              <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                {opt.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <ChevronDown
            size={18}
            className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft"
          />
        </div>
        {error ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{error}</p>
        ) : helperText ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{helperText}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }
);

import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type ShimmerProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>;

/** Base shimmer block - grayscale sweep, works in light + dark theme. */
export function Shimmer({ className, ...props }: ShimmerProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "relative overflow-hidden rounded-control bg-paper-dim",
        "before:absolute before:inset-0 before:-translate-x-full",
        "before:bg-gradient-to-r before:from-transparent before:via-white/60 before:to-transparent",
        "before:animate-shimmer dark:before:via-white/10",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export function ShimmerText({ lines = 3, lastLineWidth = "60%" }: { lines?: number; lastLineWidth?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      {Array.from({ length: lines }).map((_, i) => (
        <Shimmer key={i} className="h-3.5" style={{ width: i === lines - 1 ? lastLineWidth : "100%" }} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerKpiCard() {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5">
      <Shimmer className="mb-4 h-9 w-9 rounded-full" />
      <Shimmer className="mb-2 h-6 w-20" />
      <Shimmer className="h-3.5 w-28" />
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerTableRow({ columns = 5 }: { columns?: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 border-b border-line px-4 py-3.5">
      {Array.from({ length: columns }).map((_, i) => (
        <Shimmer key={i} className="h-3.5 flex-1" />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerTable({ rows = 6, columns = 5 }: { rows?: number; columns?: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
      {Array.from({ length: rows }).map((_, i) => (
        <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={columns} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerChart() {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
      <Shimmer className="mb-6 h-4 w-32" />
      <Shimmer className="h-[220px] w-full" />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\ThemeToggle.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Sun, Moon } from "lucide-react";
import { useThemeStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export function ThemeToggle({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { theme, toggleTheme } = useThemeStore();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => setMounted(true), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return;
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);
  }, [theme, mounted]);

  if (!mounted) {
    return <div className={cn("h-9 w-9 shrink-0 rounded-control", className)} />;
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={toggleTheme}
      aria-label={theme === "light" ? "Switch to dark mode" : "Switch to light mode"}
      className={cn(
        "flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim",
        className
      )}
    >
      {theme === "light" ? <Moon size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} /> : <Sun size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
    </button>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: packages\ui\src\Button.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";
import { forwardRef, useState } from "react";
import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes, CSSProperties } from "react";

type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger" | "brand";
type ButtonSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";

type ButtonProps = ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  loading?: boolean;
};

const SIZE_CLASSES: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
  sm: "h-8 px-3 text-[12.5px]",
  md: "h-9 px-4 text-[13px]",
  lg: "h-11 px-5 text-[14px]"
};

type VariantStyle = {
  bg?: string;
  hoverBg?: string;
  bgImage?: string;
  hoverBgImage?: string;
  text: string;
  border: string;
  glow?: string;
};

const VARIANT_STYLE: Record<ButtonVariant, VariantStyle> = {
  primary: {
    bg: "var(--color-ink)",
    hoverBg: "var(--color-ink-soft)",
    text: "var(--color-paper)",
    border: "var(--color-ink)"
  },
  secondary: {
    bg: "var(--color-card)",
    hoverBg: "var(--color-paper-dim)",
    text: "var(--color-ink)",
    border: "var(--color-line-strong)"
  },
  ghost: {
    bg: "transparent",
    hoverBg: "var(--color-paper-dim)",
    text: "var(--color-ink-soft)",
    border: "transparent"
  },
  danger: {
    bg: "var(--color-error)",
    hoverBg: "var(--color-error)",
    text: "var(--color-paper)",
    border: "var(--color-error)"
  },
  brand: {
    bgImage: "var(--color-accent-gradient)",
    hoverBgImage: "var(--color-accent-gradient)",
    text: "#ffffff",
    border: "transparent",
    glow: "0 0 32px -6px rgba(124,58,237,0.55)"
  }
};

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(function Button(
  {
    variant = "primary",
    size = "md",
    className = "",
    type = "button",
    style,
    disabled,
    loading = false,
    children,
    onMouseDown,
    onMouseUp,
    ...props
  },
  ref
) {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState(false);
  const [pressed, setPressed] = useState(false);

  const v = VARIANT_STYLE[variant];
  const isDisabled = disabled || loading;

  const usesGradient = Boolean(v.bgImage);

  const dynamicStyle: CSSProperties = {
    backgroundColor: usesGradient ? undefined : isDisabled ? v.bg : hover ? v.hoverBg : v.bg,
    backgroundImage: usesGradient ? (hover ? v.hoverBgImage ?? v.bgImage : v.bgImage) : undefined,
    color: v.text,
    borderColor: v.border,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderStyle: "solid",
    opacity: isDisabled ? 0.6 : variant === "danger" && hover ? 0.9 : 1,
    filter: !isDisabled && hover && usesGradient ? "brightness(1.08)" : "none",
    transform: !isDisabled && hover ? (pressed ? "translateY(0) scale(0.97)" : "translateY(-1px)") : "translateY(0)",
    boxShadow: !isDisabled && hover ? (v.glow ?? "0 4px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.18)") : v.glow ? "0 0 24px -8px rgba(124,58,237,0.35)" : "none",
    transition:
      "background-color 150ms ease-out, filter 150ms ease-out, transform 120ms ease-out, box-shadow 150ms ease-out, opacity 150ms ease-out",
    cursor: isDisabled ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
    ...style
  };

  const classes = [
    "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-control font-medium whitespace-nowrap outline-none",
    "focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-ink",
    SIZE_CLASSES[size],
    className
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(" ");

  return (
    <button
      ref={ref}
      type={type}
      disabled={isDisabled}
      aria-busy={loading}
      className={classes}
      style={dynamicStyle}
      onMouseEnter={() => setHover(true)}
      onMouseLeave={() => {
        setHover(false);
        setPressed(false);
      }}
      onMouseDown={(e) => {
        setPressed(true);
        onMouseDown?.(e);
      }}
      onMouseUp={(e) => {
        setPressed(false);
        onMouseUp?.(e);
      }}
      {...props}
    >
      {loading ? "Working..." : children}
    </button>
  );
});

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { HelpCircle } from "lucide-react";

type HelpTooltipProps = {
  text: string;
  className?: string;
};

export function HelpTooltip({ text, className = "" }: HelpTooltipProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    }
    if (open) document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className={`relative inline-flex ${className}`} ref={ref}>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          setOpen((v) => !v);
        }}
        onMouseEnter={() => setOpen(true)}
        onMouseLeave={() => setOpen(false)}
        className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
        aria-label="Help"
      >
        <HelpCircle size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="absolute left-0 top-[calc(100%+6px)] z-20 w-[260px] rounded-control border border-line bg-card p-3 text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft shadow-lg"
        >
          {text}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { RotateCw } from "lucide-react";

type RefreshButtonProps = {
  className?: string;
  title?: string;
};

export function RefreshButton({ className = "", title = "Refresh" }: RefreshButtonProps) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [spinning, setSpinning] = useState(false);

  const handleRefresh = async () => {
    if (spinning) return;
    setSpinning(true);
    await queryClient.invalidateQueries();
    window.setTimeout(() => setSpinning(false), 500);
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleRefresh}
      title={title}
      aria-label={title}
      className={`flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:border-line-strong hover:text-ink ${className}`}
    >
      <RotateCw size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className={spinning ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
    </button>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { MoreVertical } from "lucide-react";

export type RowAction = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  onClick: () => void;
  tone?: "default" | "danger";
  disabled?: boolean;
};

type RowActionsMenuProps = {
  actions: RowAction[];
  ariaLabel?: string;
};

export function RowActionsMenu({ actions, ariaLabel = "Row actions" }: RowActionsMenuProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    }
    if (open) {
      document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    }
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block" ref={ref}>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          setOpen((v) => !v);
        }}
        aria-label={ariaLabel}
        className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
      >
        <MoreVertical size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="absolute right-0 top-[calc(100%+4px)] z-30 w-[190px] overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-lg"
        >
          <div className="flex flex-col p-1.5">
            {actions.map((action) => (
              <button
                key={action.id}
                type="button"
                disabled={action.disabled}
                onClick={() => {
                  action.onClick();
                  setOpen(false);
                }}
                className={`flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-3 py-2 text-left text-[13px] transition-colors duration-100 ease-out disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40 ${
                  action.tone === "danger"
                    ? "text-error hover:bg-error-tint"
                    : "text-ink hover:bg-paper-dim"
                }`}
              >
                {action.icon}
                {action.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\Switch.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

type SwitchProps = {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: () => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  label?: string;
  className?: string;
};

export function Switch({ checked, onChange, disabled = false, label, className = "" }: SwitchProps) {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState(false);

  const trackStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    position: "relative",
    display: "inline-flex",
    alignItems: "center",
    width: 44,
    height: 24,
    borderRadius: 999,
    border: "1px solid",
    borderColor: checked ? "var(--color-ink)" : "var(--color-line-strong)",
    backgroundColor: checked ? "var(--color-ink)" : "var(--color-paper-dim)",
    opacity: disabled ? 0.4 : hover ? 0.85 : 1,
    cursor: disabled ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
    transition: "background-color 180ms ease-out, border-color 180ms ease-out, opacity 150ms ease-out",
    outline: "none",
    padding: 0,
    flexShrink: 0
  };

  const knobStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 2,
    left: checked ? 22 : 2,
    width: 18,
    height: 18,
    borderRadius: "50%",
    backgroundColor: "var(--color-paper)",
    boxShadow: "0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",
    transition: "left 180ms ease-out"
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      aria-label={label}
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={onChange}
      onMouseEnter={() => setHover(true)}
      onMouseLeave={() => setHover(false)}
      style={trackStyle}
      className={className}
    >
      <span style={knobStyle} />
    </button>
  );
}

FILE: packages\utils\src\formatting\currency.ts

const CURRENCY_LOCALE: Record<string, string> = {
  USD: "en-US",
  PKR: "en-PK",
  GBP: "en-GB"
};

export function formatCurrency(amount: number, currency: string = "USD"): string {
  const locale = CURRENCY_LOCALE[currency] ?? "en-US";
  return new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    minimumFractionDigits: 2
  }).format(amount);
}

export function formatCompactNumber(value: number): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    notation: "compact",
    maximumFractionDigits: 1
  }).format(value);
}



================================================================================
FILE: packages\utils\src\formatting\date.ts
================================================================================
export function formatDate(input: string | Date, locale: string = "en-US"): string {
  const date = typeof input === "string" ? new Date(input) : input;
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
    year: "numeric",
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric"
  }).format(date);
}

export function formatRelativeTime(input: string | Date): string {
  const date = typeof input === "string" ? new Date(input) : input;
  const diffMs = date.getTime() - Date.now();
  const diffMinutes = Math.round(diffMs / 60000);

  const rtf = new Intl.RelativeTimeFormat("en", { numeric: "auto" });

  if (Math.abs(diffMinutes) < 60) return rtf.format(diffMinutes, "minute");
  const diffHours = Math.round(diffMinutes / 60);
  if (Math.abs(diffHours) < 24) return rtf.format(diffHours, "hour");
  const diffDays = Math.round(diffHours / 24);
  return rtf.format(diffDays, "day");
}

export function formatDuration(totalSeconds: number): string {
  const hours = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60);
  if (hours > 0) return `${hours}h ${minutes}m`;
  return `${minutes}m`;
}

import { z } from "zod";

export const loginSchema = z.object({
  email: z.string().email("Enter a valid email address"),
  password: z.string().min(8, "Password must be at least 8 characters")
});
export type LoginInput = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;

export const registerSchema = z
  .object({
    fullName: z.string().min(2, "Full name is required"),
    email: z.string().email("Enter a valid email address"),
    password: z.string().min(8, "Password must be at least 8 characters"),
    confirmPassword: z.string(),
    role: z.enum(["learner", "instructor"]).default("learner")
  })
  .refine((data) => data.password === data.confirmPassword, {
    message: "Passwords do not match",
    path: ["confirmPassword"]
  });
export type RegisterInput = z.infer<typeof registerSchema>;

export const forgotPasswordSchema = z.object({
  email: z.string().email("Enter a valid email address")
});
export type ForgotPasswordInput = z.infer<typeof forgotPasswordSchema>;

export const resetPasswordSchema = z
  .object({
    token: z.string().min(1),
    password: z.string().min(8, "Password must be at least 8 characters"),
    confirmPassword: z.string()
  })
  .refine((data) => data.password === data.confirmPassword, {
    message: "Passwords do not match",
    path: ["confirmPassword"]
  });
export type ResetPasswordInput = z.infer<typeof resetPasswordSchema>;

export const twoFactorSchema = z.object({
  code: z.string().length(6, "Enter the 6-digit code")
});
export type TwoFactorInput = z.infer<typeof twoFactorSchema>;

import { z } from "zod";

export const courseBasicsSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(5, "Title must be at least 5 characters").max(120),
  subtitle: z.string().max(180).optional(),
  categoryId: z.string().min(1, "Select a category"),
  tags: z.array(z.string()).max(10).default([]),
  language: z.string().min(1, "Select a language"),
  level: z.enum(["beginner", "intermediate", "advanced"])
});
export type CourseBasicsInput = z.infer<typeof courseBasicsSchema>;

export const coursePricingSchema = z.object({
  price: z.number().min(0, "Price cannot be negative"),
  currency: z.enum(["PKR", "USD", "GBP"]).default("USD"),
  subscriptionIncluded: z.boolean().default(false),
  couponEligible: z.boolean().default(true)
});
export type CoursePricingInput = z.infer<typeof coursePricingSchema>;

export const lessonSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(3, "Lesson title is required"),
  type: z.enum(["video", "text", "quiz", "resource"]),
  content: z.string().optional(),
  durationSeconds: z.number().int().min(0).optional()
});
export type LessonInput = z.infer<typeof lessonSchema>;

export const moduleSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(3, "Module title is required"),
  lessons: z.array(lessonSchema).default([])
});
export type ModuleInput = z.infer<typeof moduleSchema>;

export const curriculumSchema = z.object({
  modules: z.array(moduleSchema).min(1, "Add at least one module")
});
export type CurriculumInput = z.infer<typeof curriculumSchema>;

FILE: packages\store\src\useCartStore.ts
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type CartItem = {
  courseId: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
};

export type CouponState = {
  code: string;
  percentOff: number;
} | null;

// NOTE: demo-only coupon codes, validated client-side. Replace with a real
// Coupons API call once the backend (Doc 3) is available.
const KNOWN_COUPONS: Record<string, number> = {
  SAACHI10: 10,
  SAACHI25: 25
};

type CartState = {
  items: CartItem[];
  coupon: CouponState;
  addItem: (item: CartItem) => void;
  removeItem: (courseId: string) => void;
  clear: () => void;
  applyCoupon: (code: string) => { success: boolean; message: string };
  removeCoupon: () => void;
  isInCart: (courseId: string) => boolean;
  subtotal: () => number;
  discount: () => number;
  total: () => number;
};

export const useCartStore = create<CartState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      items: [],
      coupon: null,

      addItem: (item) => {
        const exists = get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === item.courseId);
        if (exists) return;
        set({ items: [...get().items, item] });
      },

      removeItem: (courseId) => {
        set({ items: get().items.filter((i) => i.courseId !== courseId) });
      },

      clear: () => set({ items: [], coupon: null }),

      applyCoupon: (code) => {
        const normalized = code.trim().toUpperCase();
        const percentOff = KNOWN_COUPONS[normalized];
        if (!percentOff) {
          return { success: false, message: "That coupon code isn't valid." };
        }
        set({ coupon: { code: normalized, percentOff } });
        return { success: true, message: `${percentOff}% discount applied.` };
      },

      removeCoupon: () => set({ coupon: null }),

      isInCart: (courseId) => get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === courseId),

      subtotal: () => get().items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.price, 0),

      discount: () => {
        const coupon = get().coupon;
        if (!coupon) return 0;
        return Math.round(get().subtotal() * (coupon.percentOff / 100) * 100) / 100;
      },

      total: () => Math.max(0, get().subtotal() - get().discount())
    }),
    { name: "saachi-cart" }
  )
);

FILE: packages\store\src\useCommunityStore.ts
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type CommunityAnswer = {
  id: string;
  authorName: string;
  isInstructor: boolean;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  upvotes: number;
};

export type CommunityQuestion = {
  id: string;
  courseId: string;
  authorName: string;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  upvotes: number;
  answers: CommunityAnswer[];
};

const SEED_QUESTIONS: CommunityQuestion[] = [
  {
    id: "q_1",
    courseId: "crs_001",
    authorName: "Hina Malik",
    body: "For few-shot prompting, how many examples is usually enough before it stops improving results?",
    createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 30).toISOString(),
    upvotes: 6,
    answers: [
      {
        id: "a_1",
        authorName: "Sana Rizvi",
        isInstructor: true,
        body: "Usually 3-5 well-chosen examples get you most of the benefit - past that you're often better off improving instructions instead.",
        createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 20).toISOString(),
        upvotes: 9
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    id: "q_2",
    courseId: "crs_001",
    authorName: "Bilal Ahmed",
    body: "Does the prompt library template work with Claude and Gemini too, or is it ChatGPT-specific?",
    createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 10).toISOString(),
    upvotes: 2,
    answers: []
  }
];

type CommunityState = {
  questions: CommunityQuestion[];
  addQuestion: (courseId: string, authorName: string, body: string) => void;
  addAnswer: (questionId: string, authorName: string, body: string) => void;
  upvoteQuestion: (questionId: string) => void;
};

function genId(prefix: string) {
  return `${prefix}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`;
}

export const useCommunityStore = create<CommunityState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      questions: SEED_QUESTIONS,

      addQuestion: (courseId, authorName, body) => {
        const newQuestion: CommunityQuestion = {
          id: genId("q"),
          courseId,
          authorName,
          body,
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          upvotes: 0,
          answers: []
        };
        set({ questions: [newQuestion, ...get().questions] });
      },

      addAnswer: (questionId, authorName, body) => {
        set({
          questions: get().questions.map((q) =>
            q.id === questionId
              ? {
                  ...q,
                  answers: [
                    ...q.answers,
                    {
                      id: genId("a"),
                      authorName,
                      isInstructor: false,
                      body,
                      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                      upvotes: 0
                    }
                  ]
                }
              : q
          )
        });
      },

      upvoteQuestion: (questionId) => {
        set({
          questions: get().questions.map((q) =>
            q.id === questionId ? { ...q, upvotes: q.upvotes + 1 } : q
          )
        });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-community" }
  )
);


================================================================================
FILE: packages\store\src\useCorporateStore.ts
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type TeamMember = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  status: "invited" | "active";
  assignedCourseIds: string[];
  completedCourseIds: string[];
};

export type CorporateAccount = {
  companyName: string;
  seatsPurchased: number;
  seatsUsed: number;
};

const SEED_TEAM: TeamMember[] = [
  {
    id: "tm_1",
    name: "Zainab Rehman",
    email: "zainab@acmeagency.com",
    status: "active",
    assignedCourseIds: ["crs_001", "crs_002"],
    completedCourseIds: ["crs_001"]
  },
  {
    id: "tm_2",
    name: "Ali Raza",
    email: "ali@acmeagency.com",
    status: "active",
    assignedCourseIds: ["crs_003"],
    completedCourseIds: []
  },
  {
    id: "tm_3",
    name: "Mahnoor Fatima",
    email: "mahnoor@acmeagency.com",
    status: "invited",
    assignedCourseIds: [],
    completedCourseIds: []
  }
];

type CorporateState = {
  account: CorporateAccount;
  team: TeamMember[];
  inviteMember: (name: string, email: string) => void;
  removeMember: (id: string) => void;
  assignCourse: (memberId: string, courseId: string) => void;
  purchaseSeats: (count: number) => void;
};

export const useCorporateStore = create<CorporateState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      account: { companyName: "Acme Agency", seatsPurchased: 10, seatsUsed: 3 },
      team: SEED_TEAM,

      inviteMember: (name, email) => {
        const newMember: TeamMember = {
          id: `tm_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`,
          name,
          email,
          status: "invited",
          assignedCourseIds: [],
          completedCourseIds: []
        };
        set({
          team: [...get().team, newMember],
          account: { ...get().account, seatsUsed: get().account.seatsUsed + 1 }
        });
      },

      removeMember: (id) => {
        set({
          team: get().team.filter((m) => m.id !== id),
          account: { ...get().account, seatsUsed: Math.max(0, get().account.seatsUsed - 1) }
        });
      },

      assignCourse: (memberId, courseId) => {
        set({
          team: get().team.map((m) =>
            m.id === memberId && !m.assignedCourseIds.includes(courseId)
              ? { ...m, assignedCourseIds: [...m.assignedCourseIds, courseId] }
              : m
          )
        });
      },

      purchaseSeats: (count) => {
        set({ account: { ...get().account, seatsPurchased: get().account.seatsPurchased + count } });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-corporate" }
  )
);

FILE: packages\store\src\useEnrollmentStore.ts
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type EnrollmentRecord = {
  courseId: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  progressPercent: number;
  purchasedAt: string;
  completedLessonIds: string[];
};

type EnrollmentState = {
  enrollments: EnrollmentRecord[];
  isEnrolled: (courseId: string) => boolean;
  enroll: (records: Omit<EnrollmentRecord, "progressPercent" | "purchasedAt" | "completedLessonIds">[]) => void;
  markLessonComplete: (courseId: string, lessonId: string, totalLessons: number) => void;
  getEnrollment: (courseId: string) => EnrollmentRecord | undefined;
};

export const useEnrollmentStore = create<EnrollmentState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      enrollments: [],

      isEnrolled: (courseId) => get().enrollments.some((e) => e.courseId === courseId),

      getEnrollment: (courseId) => get().enrollments.find((e) => e.courseId === courseId),

      enroll: (records) => {
        const now = new Date().toISOString();
        const existingIds = new Set(get().enrollments.map((e) => e.courseId));
        const fresh = records
          .filter((r) => !existingIds.has(r.courseId))
          .map((r) => ({ ...r, progressPercent: 0, purchasedAt: now, completedLessonIds: [] as string[] }));
        if (fresh.length === 0) return;
        set({ enrollments: [...get().enrollments, ...fresh] });
      },

      markLessonComplete: (courseId, lessonId, totalLessons) => {
        set({
          enrollments: get().enrollments.map((e) => {
            if (e.courseId !== courseId) return e;
            const completedLessonIds = e.completedLessonIds.includes(lessonId)
              ? e.completedLessonIds
              : [...e.completedLessonIds, lessonId];
            const progressPercent =
              totalLessons > 0 ? Math.round((completedLessonIds.length / totalLessons) * 100) : 0;
            return { ...e, completedLessonIds, progressPercent };
          })
        });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-enrollments" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type InstructorCourseLesson = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  type: "video" | "text" | "quiz" | "resource";
};

export type InstructorCourseModule = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  lessons: InstructorCourseLesson[];
};

export type InstructorCourse = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  categoryId: string;
  language: string;
  level: "beginner" | "intermediate" | "advanced";
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  status: "draft" | "pending_review" | "published";
  modules: InstructorCourseModule[];
  createdAt: string;
  enrollmentCount: number;
  revenue: number;
  rating: number;
};

type InstructorCourseState = {
  courses: InstructorCourse[];
  addCourse: (course: InstructorCourse) => void;
  updateCourse: (id: string, patch: Partial<InstructorCourse>) => void;
  removeCourse: (id: string) => void;
};

export const useInstructorCourseStore = create<InstructorCourseState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      courses: [],
      addCourse: (course) => set({ courses: [course, ...get().courses] }),
      updateCourse: (id, patch) =>
        set({ courses: get().courses.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, ...patch } : c)) }),
      removeCourse: (id) => set({ courses: get().courses.filter((c) => c.id !== id) })
    }),
    { name: "saachi-instructor-courses" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type NotificationItem = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  read: boolean;
  type: "course" | "system" | "community" | "payment";
};

const SEED_NOTIFICATIONS: NotificationItem[] = [
  {
    id: "ntf_1",
    title: "Welcome to Saachi AI",
    body: "Your account is ready. Explore practical AI courses to get started.",
    createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 2).toISOString(),
    read: false,
    type: "system"
  },
  {
    id: "ntf_2",
    title: "New reply in Prompt Engineering Fundamentals",
    body: "An instructor answered your question in the course Q&A.",
    createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 26).toISOString(),
    read: false,
    type: "community"
  },
  {
    id: "ntf_3",
    title: "Course completed",
    body: "You finished all lessons in a course. Your certificate is ready.",
    createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 72).toISOString(),
    read: true,
    type: "course"
  }
];

type NotificationState = {
  items: NotificationItem[];
  markAllRead: () => void;
  markRead: (id: string) => void;
};

export const useNotificationStore = create<NotificationState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      items: SEED_NOTIFICATIONS,
      markAllRead: () => set({ items: get().items.map((n) => ({ ...n, read: true })) }),
      markRead: (id) =>
        set({ items: get().items.map((n) => (n.id === id ? { ...n, read: true } : n)) })
    }),
    { name: "saachi-notifications" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type OrderLineItem = {
  courseId: string;
  title: string;
  price: number;
};

export type Order = {
  id: string;
  items: OrderLineItem[];
  subtotal: number;
  discount: number;
  total: number;
  currency: string;
  paymentMethod: string;
  createdAt: string;
  status: "paid" | "refunded";
};

type OrderState = {
  orders: Order[];
  addOrder: (order: Omit<Order, "id" | "createdAt" | "status">) => Order;
};

export const useOrderStore = create<OrderState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      orders: [],
      addOrder: (order) => {
        const newOrder: Order = {
          ...order,
          id: `ORD-${Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()}`,
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          status: "paid"
        };
        set({ orders: [newOrder, ...get().orders] });
        return newOrder;
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-orders" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type ActivePromotion = {
  courseId: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  tier: "bronze" | "silver" | "gold";
  purchasedAt: string;
  expiresAt: string;
};

type PromotionState = {
  active: ActivePromotion[];
  purchase: (courseId: string, courseTitle: string, tier: ActivePromotion["tier"]) => void;
};

export const usePromotionStore = create<PromotionState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      active: [],
      purchase: (courseId, courseTitle, tier) => {
        const now = new Date();
        const expires = new Date(now.getTime() + 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 14);
        set({
          active: [
            ...get().active.filter((p) => p.courseId !== courseId),
            { courseId, courseTitle, tier, purchasedAt: now.toISOString(), expiresAt: expires.toISOString() }
          ]
        });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-promotions" }
  )
);

  theme: ThemeMode;
  setTheme: (theme: ThemeMode) => void;
  toggleTheme: () => void;
};

export const useThemeStore = create<ThemeState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      theme: "light",
      setTheme: (theme) => set({ theme }),
      toggleTheme: () => set({ theme: get().theme === "light" ? "dark" : "light" })

    { name: "saachi-theme" }
  )

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type WishlistItem = {
  courseId: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  rating: number;
  ratingCount: number;
};

type WishlistState = {
  items: WishlistItem[];
  toggle: (item: WishlistItem) => void;
  remove: (courseId: string) => void;
  isWishlisted: (courseId: string) => boolean;
};

export const useWishlistStore = create<WishlistState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      items: [],

      toggle: (item) => {
        const exists = get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === item.courseId);
        if (exists) {
          set({ items: get().items.filter((i) => i.courseId !== item.courseId) });
        } else {
          set({ items: [...get().items, item] });
        }
      },

      remove: (courseId) => {
        set({ items: get().items.filter((i) => i.courseId !== courseId) });
      },

      isWishlisted: (courseId) => get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === courseId)
    }),
    { name: "saachi-wishlist" }
  )
);

FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAdminCourses.ts

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_COURSES,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_CATEGORIES,
  type AdminCourse,
  type AdminCourseStatus,
  type AdminCategory
} from "../dummy-data-admin-courses";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Admin-Courses / Categories API (Doc 3).
let COURSES_STATE: AdminCourse[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_COURSES];
let CATEGORIES_STATE: AdminCategory[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_CATEGORIES];

export const adminCourseKeys = {
  all: ["admin", "courses"] as const,
  moderationQueue: ["admin", "courses", "moderation-queue"] as const,
  catalog: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "courses", "catalog", filters] as const,
  categories: ["admin", "categories"] as const
};

export function useAdminModerationQueue() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.moderationQueue,
    queryFn: async () => delay(COURSES_STATE.filter((c) => c.status === "pending_review"))
  });
}

export type AdminCourseCatalogFilters = {
  search?: string;
  categoryId?: string;
  status?: string;
};

export function useAdminCourseCatalog(filters: AdminCourseCatalogFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.catalog(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...COURSES_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter((c) => c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.instructorName.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      }
      if (filters.categoryId) items = items.filter((c) => c.categoryId === filters.categoryId);
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((c) => c.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useModerateCourse() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({
      id,
      decision,
      reason
    }: {
      id: string;
      decision: "published" | "rejected";
      reason?: string;
    }) => {
      COURSES_STATE = COURSES_STATE.map((c) =>
        c.id === id ? { ...c, status: decision as AdminCourseStatus, rejectionReason: reason } : c
      );
      return delay({ id, decision }, 400);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useToggleCourseFlag() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, field }: { id: string; field: "featured" | "pinned" }) => {
      COURSES_STATE = COURSES_STATE.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, [field]: !c[field] } : c));
      return delay({ id, field }, 200);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useAdminCategoryList() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...CATEGORIES_STATE])
  });
}

export function useCreateCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { name: string; tags: string[] }) => {
      const slug = input.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/(^-|-$)/g, "");
      const created: AdminCategory = { id: slug, name: input.name, slug, courseCount: 0, tags: input.tags };
      CATEGORIES_STATE = [...CATEGORIES_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdateCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, name, tags }: { id: string; name: string; tags: string[] }) => {
      CATEGORIES_STATE = CATEGORIES_STATE.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, name, tags } : c));
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

export function useDeleteCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) => {
      CATEGORIES_STATE = CATEGORIES_STATE.filter((c) => c.id !== id);
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}
